====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If the insured is diagnosed with a terminal illness and accesses the Terminal Illness Accelerated Death Benefit, how much can be advanced and what is left for beneficiaries?
CATEGORY: CAT4_MULTIHOP_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: Up to 95% of the death benefit ($712,500) can be advanced (minimum $10,000, maximum $500,000), with the advance reduced by an actuarial discount. The remaining death benefit is paid to beneficiaries upon death.
EVIDENCE: RIDER 2: ACCELERATED DEATH BENEFIT - TERMINAL ILLNESS / May access up to 95% of death benefit while living / Minimum advance: $10,000 / Maximum advance: $500,000 / Benefit reduced by actuarial discount (approximately 8% per year) / Remaining death benefit paid to beneficiaries upon death
====================================================================================================

--- GOAL PLAN ---
rewritten_query: What is the maximum amount that can be advanced under a Terminal Illness Accelerated Death Benefit, and how much of the death benefit remains for beneficiaries after such an advance?
reasoning: This query is asking about a specific financial benefit within an insurance policy — the Terminal Illness Accelerated Death Benefit (TIA DB). It requires two pieces of information: (1) how much can be advanced to the insured upon diagnosis of a terminal illness, and (2) what portion of the death benefit remains for beneficiaries. This is a multi-hop query because the answer depends on understanding both the policy’s terms (e.g., percentage or maximum amount that can be accessed) and the consequence of that access (i.e., the reduction in the remaining death benefit). The key terms are "Terminal Illness Accelerated Death Benefit," "insured," and "beneficiaries." The first goal is to determine the maximum amount that can be advanced under this benefit. The second goal is to determine how much remains for beneficiaries after the advance, which depends on the first answer.

  [1] Determine how much of the death benefit remains for beneficiaries after the advance is made.  (key_terms=['beneficiaries', 'remaining death benefit'], depends_on_previous=True)
